Thus problem is different: minimize coefficients of P(x)^2, i.e., polynomial square. This is like designing polynomial with nonnegative coefficients and bounded square coefficients. Not density; convolution coefficient is sum over pairs summing to k, like multiplication of probability mass on line; it's distribution of sum of two independent draws from p. Now, can we do even better by optimizing the cosine family for each n exactly and then polishing. Let's design robust algorithm:
1. Define function sample_cosine(n, r, a, phi): returns list p_i = (1 + a*cos(2π r i/n + phi))/n, clipped to ≥0 (if a<=1 no need). For sum exactly unless numerical; normalize to sum 1.

Exact formula for p_i = (1 + a cosθ_i+φ)/n (θ=2πr/n).
For k=0..n-1, x=k+1 maybe.
B_k = 2 Re(e^{iφ}(1 - e^{iθ(k+1)})/(1 - e^{iθ})). If r=0? not.
C_k = (k+1)/2 cos(θk+2φ) + Re(e^{-iθk} (1 - e^{i2θ(k+1)})/(1 - e^{i2θ})).
h_k=(k+1 + a B_k + a^2 C_k)/n^2.
Score =2n max h_k.
Need handle denominator near zero: r mod n =0 (θ=0) uniform: score=2. r = n/2? θ=π, 1-e^{iθ}=2 ok; 1-e^{i2θ}=0 for 2θ=2π, formula fails. But r not n/2 if we avoid. If n even and r=n/2, frequency alternating; can handle separately or skip. Choose r small < n/2.
We can vectorize:
ks = np.arange(n)
m = ks+1

Let's implement `cosine_h(k, n, r, a, phi)` vectorized? To avoid memory for many params, compute k vector once per n,r, θ. For each param, compute B,C. B depends phi and a separately linear; C depends a^2 and phi. We can precompute for r,n:
ks = arange(n); m=ks+1; theta=2πr/n.
For B: B(phi) = 2 Re(e^{iφ} Z_m), where Z_m=(1-e^{iθm})/(1-e^{iθ}). Precompute Zm complex. For C: C = m/2 cos(theta ks +2φ) + Re( e^{-iθks} (1-e^{i2θm})/(1-e^{i2θ}) ). The second term independent of phi! Let's call D_k. First term A2_k(φ)= m/2 cos(theta ks +2φ)= (m/2)(cos(theta ks) cos2φ - sin(theta ks) sin2φ). The independent D_k can be precomputed. So each evaluate very cheap:
h = (m + a*(2 Re(e^{iφ}Zm)) + a^2*(D + (m/2)cos(theta ks +2φ)))/n^2.
We can compute B array each param: b=2*real(exp(iφ)*Zm). Or precompute Zm_re, Zm_im: b=2*(cosφ*Zre - sinφ*Zim). C2 = D + 0.5*m*(cos2φ*cos(θks) - sin2φ*sin(θks)). Precompute U=0.5*m*cos(θks), V=0.5*m*sin(θks), D. Then score=2n*max(m + a*b + a^2*(D + cos2φ*U - sin2φ*V))/n^2.
This is O(n) with real ops. Very fast.
We can also handle r where 1-e^{i2θ} near zero: if sin(θ)?? r = n/2. Skip.
Precompute for n,r:
if r % n ==0: uniform score 2.
den = 1 - exp(iθ)
Zm = (1 - exp(iθ*m))/den
Zre=Zm.real, Zim=Zm.imag
D = real(exp(-iθ*ks)*(1 - exp(i2θ*m))/(1 - exp(i2θ)))
U,V.
Memory for n=32768: arrays ~ 0.26MB each, fine.

Then score for a vector: max(m + a*b2 + a^2*c2) vectorized over a. If we have a grid length 32, we can compute matrix (n x na) maybe memory 32768*32 ~1M, fine. But repeated. Or loop a.

Let's optimize cosine parameters exactly:
Function `precompute_cosine(n, r)` returns dict with arrays m, Zre, Zim, D, U, V, theta.
Need handle r=0? no.
Compute `den = 1 - exp(1j*theta)`. If abs(den) small (θ≈2πr/n with r=0 mod n) skip.
`em = exp(1j*theta*m)`; Zm=(1-em)/den.
`den2 = 1 - exp(2j*theta)`; if abs small (r=n/2) alternative: D? If 2θ=0 mod 2π, the sum ∑ e^{i2θi}=m. For r=n/2, θ=π, 2θ=2π. D = real(e^{-iπ k} * m) = m * cos(πk). We can handle but skip r=n/2.
D = real(exp(-1j*theta*ks)*(1 - exp(2j*theta*m))/(den2)).
U = 0.5*m*cos(theta*ks)
V = 0.5*m*sin(theta*ks)

Score evaluation:
```
def score_cosine(pre, a, phi):
    b = 2*(math.cos(phi)*pre['Zre'] - math.sin(phi)*pre['Zim'])
    c = pre['D'] + math.cos(2*phi)*pre['U'] - math.sin(2*phi)*pre['V']
    h = (pre['m'] + a*b + a*a*c) / (pre['n']*pre['n'])
    # due to numeric, h could negative? max
    return 2.0*pre['n']*np.max(h), h
```
We can avoid dividing by n^2 inside max: score = 2.0*(max(m + a*b + a^2*c))/n. Since 2n * (max/n^2) = 2*max/n. Good. `num = m + a*b + a2*c`, score = 2.0*np.max(num)/n.
This is faster. For k up to n-1, num ~n. score ~2.
Use `np.max(num)`.
Need ensure p nonnegative: a≤1. If a=1, some p zero; num should be nonnegative. Numeric maybe small negative; max unaffected unless all? no.

`objective_fft(seq)`:
```
def objective_fft(seq):
    a = np.clip(np.asarray(seq, float),0,1000)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=0: return inf
    a=a/s  # probability
    c=fft_convolve_self(a)
    return float(2.0*a.shape[0]*c.max())
```

def run_lse(a_init, n, S, max_time, rng, beta_start=10.0, beta_end=500.0, step_factor=0.5):
    a = safe_normalize(a_init,n,S)
    y = a.copy(); t=1.0; step=1.0/(2*beta_start)
    best=a.copy(); best_score=objective_internal(a) # c.max
    start=...
    it=0
    while time<max_time:
       it+=1
       beta = beta_start + (beta_end-beta_start)*(1 - remaining)?? or min(beta_end, beta_start* ...)
       c = fft_convolve_self(y)
       score=c.max(); if score<best_score: update
       # softmax weights over c
       cmax=c.max(); exp_beta=np.exp(beta*(c-cmax)); total=exp_beta.sum(); if total<... w=zeros
       w=exp_beta/total
       grad = 2*fft_corr(w,a)
       # if grad norm small break
       step = 1.0/(2.0*beta) * step_factor? previous 1/(2β). We can adapt.
       x = project_simplex(y - step*grad, S)
       # momentum
       t_new=0.5*(1+sqrt(1+4t^2)); y = x + ((t-1)/t_new)*(x-a); y=np.maximum(y,0); a=x; t=t_new
       # restart if x score not much better? We can check every 10 iters and if not improved for 500, reset momentum, maybe increase noise.
```

def resize_interp(x, m):
   if len==0 return ones
   old=np.clip(x,0,1000); old/=old.sum()
   old_pos = (np.arange(len(old))-0.5)/(len(old))
   new_pos = (np.arange(m)-0.5)/m
   y=np.interp(new_pos, old_pos, old)
   y=np.maximum(y,0); y/=y.sum()
   return y
```

Search implementation details:
```
def search_cosine_params(n, r, time_limit):
    pre=precompute...
    if pre is None: return None
    best_score=np.inf; best_a=0.707; best_phi=np.pi/2
    # coarse grid
    a_vals=np.array([0.3,0.4,0.5,0.6,0.65,0.7,0.75,0.8,0.85,0.9,0.95,1.0])
    phi_vals=np.linspace(0.0, 2*np.pi, 96, endpoint=False)
    m=pre['m']; Zre=...; ...
    # For each phi, vectorized over a
    for phi in phi_vals:
        cphi=math.cos(phi); sphi=math.sin(phi); c2=math.cos(2*phi); s2=math.sin(2*phi)
        b = 2.0*(cphi*Zre - sphi*Zim)
        c = D + c2*U - s2*V
        # matrix: shape (n, len(a_vals))
        na=len(a_vals)
        # To avoid large allocation repeatedly, maybe use loop if n*na > 1e6? 16k*12=196k fine.
        A = a_vals[None, :]
        AA = A*A
        # broadcasting
        num = m[:, None] + A * b[:, None] + AA * c[:, None]
        scores = 2.0*np.max(num, axis=0)/float(n)
        idx=int(np.argmin(scores))
        sc=float(scores[idx]); aa=float(a_vals[idx])
        if sc<best_score: ...
        # also maybe check neighbors
    # refine around best
    for _ in range(2):
        a_min=max(0.1,best_a-0.15); a_min=min(1.0,best_a+0.15)
        a_vals=np.linspace(a_min, a_max, 41)
        phi_min=(best_phi - 1.6*np.pi/9?) Use angular search. Since phi 0..2π periodic. We can sample phi around best with mask.
        phi_vals = (best_phi + (np.linspace(-0.25,0.25,64))*np.pi) % (2*np.pi)
        ... vectorized
    return best_a,best_phi,best_score

FISTA function details:
```
def run_fista_lse(a_scaled, n, S, max_time, rng, beta_start=20.0, beta_end=800.0, noise_amp=0.0):
   a=safe_normalize(a_scaled,n,S)
   y=a.copy(); t=1.0; it=0
   best=a.copy(); best_score = max(fft_convolve_self(a))
   start=time.monotonic(); last_improve=0; step=1/(2*beta_start)
   beta=beta_start
   while time<max_time:
      it+=1
      beta=min(beta_end, beta_start*(1+0.002*it)?) or linearly based on progress.
      c=fft_convolve_self(y)
      score=c.max()
      if score<best_score: best=...; last_improve=0
      else last_improve+=1
      # softmax
      cmax=c.max(); expb=np.exp(beta*(c-cmax)); total=expb.sum()
      if total>...: w=expb/total else w=zeros
      grad=fft_corr(w,a) # function includes factor 2
      # if grad norm small break
      step=1.0/(2.0*beta) # maybe scale by (1+0.0001it) denominator
      x=project_simplex(y - step*grad, S)
      # check x every few
      if it % 5 ==0:
           cx=fft_convolve_self(x); sx=cx.max()
           if sx<best_score: best=x; best_score=sx; last_improve=0
      t_new=...
      y = x + ((t-1)/t_new)*(x-a); y=np.maximum(y,0)
      a=x; t=t_new
      # restart if stagnant
      if last_improve>3000 and it%5==0:
           a=best.copy(); y=a; t=1.0
           if noise_amp>0: y=project_simplex(y + noise_amp*rng.normal(...), S)
           last_improve=0
   return best,best_score
```

def conv_and_grad_lse(a, beta):
    n=len(a); size=3*n-2; F=next_pow2(size)
    A=fft(a,F)
    c=np.real(ifft(A*A))[:2*n-1]
    cmax=c.max(); expb=np.exp(beta*(c-cmax)); total=expb.sum()
    if total<...: w=zeros
    else w=expb/total
    W=fft(w,F)
    grad=2*np.real(ifft(W*np.conj(A)))[:n]
    return c, grad
```

def conv_toppk_grad(a, top_k):
   n=len; F=next_pow2(3n-2); A=fft(a,F); c=real(ifft(A*A))[:2n-1]

Potential bug in `project_simplex`: For large n and S, works. But if v contains very small negative due to momentum, max. The algorithm ensures sum exactly S.

```
def project_simplex(v, S):
    v=np.asarray(v,float)
    n=len(v)
    if n==0: return v
    # if S <=0 return zeros
    w=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cumsum=np.cumsum(w)
    css=np.ones(n)
    theta=(cumsum - S)/css
    cond=theta<=w
    if not np.any(cond): rho=n
    else: rho=np.where(cond)[0].max()+1
    theta=(cumsum[rho-1]-S)/rho
    return np.maximum(v-theta, 0.0)
```
Some numerical: if S close to sum? okay.

`safe_normalize`:
```
def safe_normalize(a,n,S):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).reshape(-1)
    if a.size!=n: resize? not
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=1e-15: return np.full(n,S/n)
    return a*(S/s)
```

`next_pow2(size)`:
```
if size<=1: return 1
return 1 << (size-1).bit_length()
```

C(x)=∫_0^x [1 + A cos(2πu+φ)] [1 + A cos(2π(x-u)+φ)] du, A=a.
Expand:
Term0: x.
Term1: A ∫_0^x cos(2πu+φ) du + A ∫_0^x cos(2πv+φ) dv (v=x-u) = 2A I, where I=[sin(2πu+φ)/(2π)]_0^x = (sin(2πx+φ)-sinφ)/(2π).
So term1 = A/π (sin(2πx+φ)-sinφ). This was right.
Term2: A^2 ∫_0^x cos(2πu+φ) cos(2π(x-u)+φ) du.
Let first α=2πu+φ. Second β=2πx-2πu+φ. Sum α+β=2πx+2φ (constant). Difference α-β=4πu-2πx.
Product =1/2[cos(2πx+2φ)+cos(4πu-2πx)]. (No +? Difference α-β = (2πu+φ)-(2πx-2πu+φ)=4πu-2πx. Yes.)
Integral = 1/2[ x cos(2πx+2φ) + ∫_0^x cos(4πu-2πx) du ].
Integral ∫ cos(4πu-2πx) du = [sin(4πu-2πx)/(4π)]_0^x = (sin(4πx-2πx) - sin(-2πx))/(4π) = (sin2πx + sin2πx)/(4π)= sin2πx/(2π).
Times 1/2 => sin2πx/(4π). So term2 = A^2[ x/2 cos(2πx+2φ) + sin2πx/(4π)]. This is what we had. Good.

FISTA LSE code details with restart:
```
def run_fista_lse(a_prob, n, S, max_time, rng, beta_start=5.0, beta_end=300.0, noise_frac=0.005):
    a=safe_normalize(a_prob,n,S)
    y=a.copy(); t=1.0
    best=a.copy(); best_score=conv_max(a) # c.max
    start=time.monotonic(); it=0; last=0
    max_iter_no_improve=5000
    while time.monotonic()-start < max_time:
       it+=1
       beta = min(beta_end, beta_start*(1.0 + 0.002*it))
       c, grad = conv_and_grad_lse(y, beta)
       score=c.max()
       if score < best_score:
           best_score=score; best=y.copy(); last=0
       else:
           last+=1
       if grad==0: break
       step=1.0/(2.0*beta)
       # optional scale step by (1/(1+0.0001it))
       step /= (1.0 + 0.0002*it)
       x=project_simplex(y - step*grad, S)
       # momentum
       t_new=0.5*(1+sqrt(1+4*t*t))
       y = x + ((t-1)/t_new)*(x-a)
       y = np.maximum(y,0)
       a=x; t=t_new
       if it % 10 ==0:
           cx=fft_convolve_self(x); sx=cx.max()
           if sx<best_score:
               best_score=sx; best=x.copy(); last=0
           elif sx > best_score*(1+1e-4) and last>max_iter_no_improve:
               # reset
               a=best.copy(); y=a.copy(); t=1.0; last=0
               # noise
               if noise_frac>0:
                   y=project_simplex(a + rng.normal(0, noise_frac*S/np.sqrt(n), n), S)
       if last > max_iter_no_improve*3:
           a=best.copy(); y=a.copy(); t=1.0; last=0
    return best, best_score
```
But `conv_and_grad_lse` uses `y` and beta. For score at x check, we use FFT. Good.
Potential issue: In FISTA, gradient should be evaluated at y, but we then compute x using y - step grad. In code, `a` is previous x; `y` is extrapolated. We update y using x and a. Good.
Score at y maybe not accepted; best uses y when score improves, but y may not be projected? y is projected by momentum and max, but sum may drift? We clip y=max but don't normalize sum. We should ensure y sum S? Momentum combination of projected x and a (both sum S) with coefficient sum 1, so sum S; max(y,0) may increase sum. Previous didn't renormalize y, only max. To be safe, after y = max(y,0), we can project_simplex(y,S)? That would kill momentum. Or renormalize sum: `s=y.sum(); if s>0: y*=S/s`. But projection after momentum more standard: x is projected; y for next gradient can be outside simplex but not too. If we clip negative, sum may increase; renormalize to S. Use `y = np.maximum(y,0); sy=y.sum(); if sy>0: y *= S/sy`. Good.

But using `score=c.max()` from y (not normalized?) y normalized. okay.

def polish_sequence(a_prob, n, deadline, rng):
    S=np.sqrt(2*n)
    a0=safe_normalize(a_prob,n,S)
    best=a0; best_score=conv_max(a0)
    remaining=deadline-now
    # allocate phases proportional to remaining maybe fixed seconds based on n
    t1=min(remaining*0.4, 1200)
    if t1>10:
       a1,s1=run_fista_lse(a0,n,S,t1,rng,beta_start=5,beta_end=200)
       if s1<best_score: ...
    remaining2=...
    if remaining2>10:
       a2,s2=run_fista_toppk(best,n,S,min(remaining2*0.35,600),rng)
       update
    if time left >10:
       a3,s3=run_fista_lse(best,n,S,min(remaining*0.3,900), rng, beta_start=30,beta_end=800)
       update
    if time left >5:
       # small noise restart toppk
    return best,best_score
```

Functions:
`next_pow2(size)`.

`fft_convolve_self(a)`:
```
n=a.size
if n==0: return np.array([])
size=2*n-1
F=next_pow2(size)
A=fft(a,F)
c=np.real(ifft(A*A))
return c[:size]
```

`conv_and_grad_lse(a, beta)`:
```
n=a.size
F=next_pow2(3*n-2)
A=fft(a,F)
c=np.real(ifft(A*A))[:2*n-1]
cmax=c.max()
expb=np.exp(beta*(c-cmax))
total=expb.sum()
if total <=1e-300:
   w=np.zeros(2*n-1)
else:
   w=expb/total
W=fft(w,F)
grad=2.0*np.real(ifft(W*np.conj(A)))[:n]
return c, grad
```
Note: `w` length 2n-1, fft pads zeros to F. Good.
Potential overflow in exp if beta large and c-cmax negative, max 0, exp≤1, no overflow. cmax maybe negative? c nonnegative but if numeric negative, c-cmax <=0. Good.

`conv_and_grad_toppk(a, top_k)`:
```
n=a.size; K=2*n-1; F=next_pow2(3*n-2)
A=fft(a,F)
c=np.real(ifft(A*A))[:K]
if top_k <=0: top_k=1
if top_k >= K: idx=np.arange(K)
else: idx=np.argpartition(c, -top_k)[-top_k:]
vals=c[idx]
vsum=vals.sum()
if vsum <=1e-300:
   weights=np.ones(top_k)/top_k
else:
   weights=vals/vsum
w=np.zeros(K)
w[idx]=weights
W=fft(w,F)
grad=2.0*np.real(ifft(W*np.conj(A)))[:n]
return c, grad
```
If some vals negative? c nonnegative. If top_k picks negative due to all negative? no.

`project_simplex` as above. Need if S=0 return zeros.

`safe_normalize(a,n,S)`:
```
arr=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
if arr.size != n:
   # if shorter/longer, truncate/pad? Better resize? But internal expects correct. We'll only call correct.
arr=np.clip(arr,0,1000)
s=arr.sum()
if s<=1e-15: return np.full(n,S/n)
return arr*(S/s)
```

`build_cosine_seq(n,r,a,phi)`:
```
i=np.arange(n, dtype=float)
theta=2*np.pi*r/n
seq=(1.0 + a*np.cos(theta*i + phi))/n
seq=np.maximum(seq,0.0)
s=seq.sum()
if s>0: seq=seq/s
return seq
```

`precompute_full_cosine(n,r)`:
```
if r <=0 or r >= n//2: return None? If r>=n, reduce? r = r % n; if r==0 return None? uniform. If r > n-r, equivalent? Cosine with r' = n-r same family reversed, score same? For full score, maybe not same? P with e^{i2πri} and e^{-i2πri} correspond to reversed sequence? Score same (max of reversed convolution? P_r(z) and P_{n-r}(z) reversed? Coeffs reversed, max same). So we can set r=min(r, n-r). If r==0: return uniform arrays? Not needed.
K=2*n-1
k=np.arange(K, dtype=float)
L=np.maximum(0.0, k - (n-1))
U=np.minimum(float(n-1), k)
m=U-L+1.0
theta=2*math.pi*r/n
den1=1.0 - np.exp(1j*theta)
Z1=np.exp(1j*theta*L)*(1.0 - np.exp(1j*theta*m))/den1
Zre=Z1.real; Zim=Z1.imag
den2=1.0 - np.exp(2j*theta)
if abs(den2)>1e-14:
    E=np.exp(1j*(2.0*theta*L - theta*k))*(1.0 - np.exp(2.0*theta*m))/den2
    G=0.5*E.real
else:
    G=0.5*m*np.cos(theta*k)
Uarr=0.5*m*np.cos(theta*k)
Varr=0.5*m*np.sin(theta*k)
return dict(n=n,K=K,m=m,Zre=Zre,Zim=Zim,U=Uarr,V=Varr,G=G)
```
Check for r=1,n=16384, theta small, den2 not zero.

`cosine_score_pre(pre, a, phi)`:
```
cphi=math.cos(phi); sphi=math.sin(phi); c2=math.cos(2*phi); s2=math.sin(2*phi)
b=2*(cphi*pre['Zre'] - sphi*pre['Zim'])
c=pre['G'] + c2*pre['U'] - s2*pre['V']
a2=a*a
hnum=pre['m'] + a*b + a2*c
return 2.0*float(np.max(hnum)) / float(pre['n'])
```

Let's code FISTA robustly:
```
def run_fista_lse(a_scaled, n, S, max_time, rng, beta_start=20.0, beta_end=400.0):
    a=safe_normalize(a_scaled,n,S)
    y=a.copy(); t=1.0
    c0=fft_convolve_self(a); best_score=float(c0.max()); best=a.copy()
    start=time.monotonic(); it=0; last=0
    while time.monotonic()-start < max_time:
       it+=1
       beta = min(beta_end, beta_start + beta_start*0.002*it) # linear factor
       c, grad = conv_and_grad_lse(y,beta)
       score=float(c.max())
       if score < best_score:
          best_score=score; best=y.copy(); last=0
       else:
          last += 1
       gnorm=np.linalg.norm(grad)
       if gnorm < 1e-14: break
       step=1.0/(2.0*beta)
       step /= (1.0+0.0002*it)
       x=project_simplex(y - step*grad, S)
       t_new=0.5*(1.0+math.sqrt(1.0+4.0*t*t))
       y = x + ((t-1.0)/t_new)*(x-a)
       y = np.maximum(y,0.0)
       sy=y.sum()
       if sy>0: y *= S/sy
       a=x; t=t_new
       if it % 10 ==0:
          cx=fft_convolve_self(x); sx=float(cx.max())
          if sx < best_score:
             best_score=sx; best=x.copy(); last=0
       if last > 4000:
          a=best.copy(); y=best.copy(); t=1.0; last=0
          noise = rng.normal(0.0, 0.005*S/math.sqrt(n), n)
          y=project_simplex(y + noise, S)
    # final ensure best normalize
    return safe_normalize(best,n,S), best_score
```
But if we update best to y when y score improves, y may have momentum and not exactly in simplex? We renormalized and nonnegative, okay. But we didn't project y, just clip and sum scale; it remains in simplex? Nonnegative and sum S, yes (clipping can break sum but rescaled). Good.

Potential issue: `best=y.copy()` before rescaling? We scale y after update? In loop, c computed for y (scaled from previous). At end we set y new scaled. If score improved for current y, best is current y scaled. Good.

def polish_sequence(a_prob, n, deadline, rng):
    if a_prob is None: return None, inf
    S=np.sqrt(2.0*n)
    a0=safe_normalize(a_prob,n,S)
    best=a0; best_score=conv_max(a0)
    now=time.monotonic()
    rem=deadline-now
    # choose segments based on n
    max_lse1 = min(rem*0.35, 900.0)
    if max_lse1 > 20:
       a1,s1=run_fista_lse(a0,n,S,max_lse1,rng,20,300)
       if s1<best_score: best,s1...
    rem=...
    max_tk = min(rem*0.3, 900)
    if >20: run toppk
    rem=...
    max_lse2 = min(rem*0.3, 900)
    if >20: run lse beta 50-800
    rem=...
    if rem>10: run toppk high k 128-512
    return safe_normalize(best,n,S), best_score
```

def refine_cosine_pre(pre, a_best, phi_best, time_limit):
   t0=time.monotonic(); remaining=time_limit
   best_score, best_a, best_phi = cosine_score_pre(pre,a_best,phi_best), a_best, phi_best
   stages = [(0.2,33,0.5,97), (0.08,33,0.18,97), (0.025,33,0.05,97)]
   for a_span,a_cnt,phi_span_frac,phi_cnt in stages:
      rem=time_limit-(time.monotonic()-t0)
      if rem<2: break
      amin=max(0.0, best_a-a_span); amax=min(1.0,best_a+a_span)
      if amax-amin <1e-5: a_vals=np.array([best_a])
      else: a_vals=np.linspace(amin,amax,a_cnt)
      phi_vals=(best_phi + math.pi*phi_span_frac*np.linspace(-1,1,phi_cnt)) % (2*math.pi)
      sc,a,phi=score_cosine_grid(pre,a_vals,phi_vals, time.monotonic(), rem)
      if sc<best_score: update
   return best_score,best_a,best_phi
```

Pseudo code exactly:
```
def run_fista_lse(a0, n, S, max_time, rng, beta_start, beta_end):
    if max_time <=0: ...
    a=safe_normalize(a0,n,S)
    y=a.copy(); t=1.0
    c=fft_convolve_self(a); best_score=float(c.max()); best=a.copy()
    start=time.monotonic(); it=0; last=0
    while time.monotonic()-start < max_time:
       it += 1
       beta = beta_start * (1.0 + 0.002*it)
       if beta > beta_end: beta = beta_end
       c, grad = conv_and_grad_lse(y,beta)
       if grad.shape[0] != n: break
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)): break
       score=float(c.max())
       if score < best_score:
          best_score=score; best=y.copy(); last=0
       else: last +=1
       gnorm=float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
       if gnorm < 1e-14: break
       step = 1.0/(2.0*beta)
       step /= (1.0 + 0.0002*it)
       x=project_simplex(y - step*grad, S)
       t_new=0.5*(1.0 + math.sqrt(1.0 + 4.0*t*t))
       y = x + ((t - 1.0)/t_new)*(x - a)
       y = np.maximum(y,0.0)
       sy=float(y.sum())
       if sy > 0.0:
          y = y * (S/sy)
       else:
          y = a.copy()
       a=x; t=t_new
       if it % 10 ==0:
          cx=fft_convolve_self(x); sx=float(cx.max())
          if sx < best_score:
             best_score=sx; best=x.copy(); last=0
       if last > 6000:
          a=best.copy(); y=best.copy(); t=1.0; last=0
          noise = rng.normal(0.0, 0.005*S/math.sqrt(n), n)
          y=project_simplex(y + noise, S)
    return safe_normalize(best,n,S), best_score
```
But if score improves at y, we set best=y; y is normalized and nonnegative, okay. But after momentum we set y new; best copy current.

Top-k similar but step uses normalized grad.

def resize_sequence(x, m):
    arr=np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    arr=np.clip(arr,0,1000)
    s=arr.sum()
    if arr.size ==0 or s <=1e-15: return None
    arr=arr/s
    old=(np.arange(arr.size)+0.5)/arr.size
    new=(np.arange(m)+0.5)/m
    y=np.interp(new, old, arr)
    y=np.maximum(y,0.0)
    sy=y.sum()
    if sy<=1e-15: return None
    return y/sy
```

```python
"""
Search for non-negative sequences minimizing
    2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / sum(a)^2.

The program uses:
1. Fast exact evaluation of a one-parameter sinusoidal family over all
   convolution lags, to obtain strong initial sequences.
2. FFT-accelerated FISTA / log-sum-exp polishing and top-k subgradient
   polishing on the simplex.
3. A final safe normalization and verification.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    from scipy.fft import fft, ifft
except Exception:
    from numpy.fft import fft, ifft


def next_pow2(size):
    if size <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (int(size) - 1).bit_length()


def fft_convolve_self(a):
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    size = 2 * n - 1
    F = next_pow2(size)
    A = fft(a, F)
    c = np.real(ifft(A * A))
    return c[:size]


def conv_and_grad_lse(a, beta):
    n = int(a.size)
    K = 2 * n - 1
    F = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    A = fft(a, F)
    c = np.real(ifft(A * A))[:K]

    cmax = float(c.max())
    expb = np.exp(beta * (c - cmax))
    total = float(expb.sum())
    if total < 1e-300:
        w = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        w = expb / total

    W = fft(w, F)
    grad = 2.0 * np.real(ifft(W * np.conj(A)))[:n]
    return c, grad


def conv_and_grad_toppk(a, top_k):
    n = int(a.size)
    K = 2 * n - 1
    F = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    A = fft(a, F)
    c = np.real(ifft(A * A))[:K]

    top_k = int(max(1, min(top_k, K)))
    if top_k >= K:
        idx = np.arange(K)
    else:
        idx = np.argpartition(c, -top_k)[-top_k:]

    vals = c[idx]
    vsum = float(vals.sum())
    if vsum < 1e-300:
        weights = np.ones(top_k, dtype=np.float64) / float(top_k)
    else:
        weights = vals / vsum

    w = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
    w[idx] = weights
    W = fft(w, F)
    grad = 2.0 * np.real(ifft(W * np.conj(A)))[:n]
    return c, grad


def conv_max_scaled(a):
    c = fft_convolve_self(a)
    return float(c.max())


def project_simplex(v, S):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(v.size)
    if n == 0:
        return v
    if S <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    w = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cumsum = np.cumsum(w)
    css = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    theta = (cumsum - S) / css
    valid = theta <= w

    if not np.any(valid):
        rho = n
    else:
        rho = int(np.max(np.where(valid)[0]) + 1)

    theta = (cumsum[rho - 1] - S) / rho
    return np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)


def safe_normalize(a, n, S):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = int(n)
    if arr.size != n:
        if arr.size > n:
            arr = arr[:n]
        else:
            tmp = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            m = min(arr.size, n)
            tmp[:m] = arr[:m]
            arr = tmp

    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.full(n, S / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    return arr * (S / s)


def obj_fft(seq):
    a = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if a.size == 0 or s <= 1e-15:
        return np.inf
    a = a / s
    c = fft_convolve_self(a)
    return float(2.0 * a.size * c.max())


def build_cosine_seq(n, r, a, phi):
    n = int(n)
    r = int(r)
    theta = 2.0 * math.pi * r / float(n)
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    seq = (1.0 + float(a) * np.cos(theta * i + float(phi))) / float(n)
    seq = np.maximum(seq, 0.0)
    s = float(seq.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        seq = np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        seq = seq / s
    return seq


def precompute_full_cosine(n, r):
    n = int(n)
    r = int(r) % n
    if r == 0:
        return None
    if r > n - r:
        r = n - r
    if r == 0:
        return None

    K = 2 * n - 1
    k = np.arange(K, dtype=np.float64)
    L = np.maximum(0.0, k - float(n - 1))
    U = np.minimum(float(n - 1), k)
    m = U - L + 1.0

    theta = 2.0 * math.pi * r / float(n)

    den1 = 1.0 - np.exp(1j * theta)
    Z1 = np.exp(1j * theta * L) * (1.0 - np.exp(1j * theta * m)) / den1
    Z1_re = Z1.real
    Z1_im = Z1.imag

    den2 = 1.0 - np.exp(2j * theta)
    if abs(den2) > 1e-14:
        E = np.exp(1j * (2.0 * theta * L - theta * k)) * (
            1.0 - np.exp(2j * theta * m)
        ) / den2
        G = 0.5 * E.real
    else:
        G = 0.5 * m * np.cos(theta * k)

    Uarr = 0.5 * m * np.cos(theta * k)
    Varr = 0.5 * m * np.sin(theta * k)

    return {
        "n": n,
        "K": K,
        "m": m,
        "Z1_re": Z1_re,
        "Z1_im": Z1_im,
        "U": Uarr,
        "V": Varr,
        "G": G,
    }


def cosine_score_pre(pre, a, phi):
    cphi = math.cos(phi)
    sphi = math.sin(phi)
    c2 = math.cos(2.0 * phi)
    s2 = math.sin(2.0 * phi)

    b = 2.0 * (cphi * pre["Z1_re"] - sphi * pre["Z1_im"])
    c = pre["G"] + c2 * pre["U"] - s2 * pre["V"]
    hnum = pre["m"] + float(a) * b + float(a) * float(a) * c
    return float(2.0 * np.max(hnum) / float(pre["n"]))


def score_cosine_grid(pre, a_vals, phi_vals, start_time=None, time_limit=None):
    best_score = np.inf
    best_a = 0.7
    best_phi = math.pi / 2.0

    t0 = time.monotonic() if start_time is None else float(start_time)
    a_vals = np.asarray(a_vals, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    phi_vals = np.asarray(phi_vals, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)

    if a_vals.size == 0 or phi_vals.size == 0:
        return best_score, best_a, best_phi

    m = pre["m"]
    Zre = pre["Z1_re"]
    Zim = pre["Z1_im"]
    U = pre["U"]
    V = pre["V"]
    G = pre["G"]
    n = float(pre["n"])

    A = a_vals[None, :]
    AA = A * A

    for phi in phi_vals:
        if time_limit is not None and (time.monotonic() - t0) > float(time_limit):
            break

        cphi = math.cos(phi)
        sphi = math.sin(phi)
        c2 = math.cos(2.0 * phi)
        s2 = math.sin(2.0 * phi)

        b = 2.0 * (cphi * Zre - sphi * Zim)
        c = G + c2 * U - s2 * V

        hnum = m[:, None] + b[:, None] * A + c[:, None] * AA
        scores = 2.0 * np.max(hnum, axis=0) / n
        idx = int(np.argmin(scores))
        sc = float(scores[idx])
        aa = float(a_vals[idx])

        if sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_a = aa
            best_phi = float(phi)

    return best_score, best_a, best_phi


def refine_cosine_pre(pre, a_best, phi_best, time_limit=60.0):
    a_best = float(a_best)
    phi_best = float(phi_best)
    best_score = cosine_score_pre(pre, a_best, phi_best)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    stages = [
        (0.20, 33, 0.50, 97),
        (0.08, 33, 0.18, 97),
        (0.03, 33, 0.05, 81),
    ]

    for a_span, a_cnt, phi_span_frac, phi_cnt in stages:
        rem = float(time_limit) - (time.monotonic() - t0)
        if rem < 2.0:
            break

        amin = max(0.0, a_best - a_span)
        amax = min(1.0, a_best + a_span)
        if amax - amin < 1e-5:
            a_vals = np.array([amin], dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            a_vals = np.linspace(amin, amax, int(a_cnt), dtype=np.float64)

        phi_vals = (
            phi_best + math.pi * phi_span_frac * np.linspace(-1.0, 1.0, int(phi_cnt))
        ) % (2.0 * math.pi)

        sc, aa, pp = score_cosine_grid(
            pre, a_vals, phi_vals, start_time=time.monotonic(), time_limit=max(1.0, rem - 1.0)
        )
        if sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            a_best = aa
            phi_best = pp

    return best_score, a_best, phi_best


def search_cosine_plan(plan, deadline):
    candidates = []
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    remaining = max(0.0, float(deadline) - t0)
    search_hard = t0 + max(15.0, min(300.0, 0.12 * remaining))

    coarse_a = np.linspace(0.10, 1.0, 15, dtype=np.float64)
    coarse_phi = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi, 65, endpoint=False, dtype=np.float64)

    for n, rs in plan:
        for r in rs:
            if time.monotonic() > search_hard:
                break

            pre = precompute_full_cosine(n, r)
            if pre is None:
                continue

            rem = float(search_hard - time.monotonic())
            if rem < 2.0:
                break

            sc, aa, pp = score_cosine_grid(pre, coarse_a, coarse_phi, start_time=time.monotonic(), time_limit=rem * 0.45)
            sc, aa, pp = refine_cosine_pre(pre, aa, pp, time_limit=max(2.0, search_hard - time.monotonic() - 1.0))

            seq = build_cosine_seq(n, r, aa, pp)
            sc_fft = obj_fft(seq)
            if np.isfinite(sc_fft):
                candidates.append((float(sc_fft), seq, int(n)))
                print("  cosine candidate n=%d r=%d a=%.6f phi=%.6f score=%.8f" %
                      (n, r, aa, pp, sc_fft))
            else:
                print("  cosine candidate n=%d r=%d failed FFT score" % (n, r))

    if time.monotonic() > search_hard:
        print("  cosine search aborted by time budget")

    return candidates


def resize_sequence(x, m):
    arr = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if arr.size == 0 or s <= 1e-15:
        return None
    arr = arr / s

    old = (np.arange(arr.size, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(arr.size)
    new = (np.arange(int(m), dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(m)
    y = np.interp(new, old, arr)
    y = np.maximum(y, 0.0)
    sy = float(y.sum())
    if sy <= 1e-15:
        return None
    return y / sy


def run_fista_lse(a0, n, S, max_time, rng, beta_start, beta_end):
    a = safe_normalize(a0, n, S)
    y = a.copy()
    t = 1.0
    c = fft_convolve_self(a)
    best_score = float(c.max())
    best = a.copy()

    start = time.monotonic()
    it = 0
    last_improve = 0

    while time.monotonic() - start < max_time:
        it += 1
        beta = beta_start * (1.0 + 0.002 * it)
        if beta > beta_end:
            beta = beta_end

        c, grad = conv_and_grad_lse(y, beta)
        if grad.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
            break

        score = float(c.max())
        if score < best_score:
            best_score = score
            best = y.copy()
            last_improve = 0
        else:
            last_improve += 1

        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
        if gnorm < 1e-14:
            break

        step = 1.0 / (2.0 * beta)
        step /= (1.0 + 0.0002 * it)

        x = project_simplex(y - step * grad, S)

        t_new = 0.5 * (1.0 + math.sqrt(1.0 + 4.0 * t * t))
        y = x + ((t - 1.0) / t_new) * (x - a)
        y = np.maximum(y, 0.0)
        sy = float(y.sum())
        if sy > 0.0:
            y *= S / sy
        else:
            y = a.copy()

        a = x
        t = t_new

        if it % 10 == 0:
            cx = fft_convolve_self(x)
            sx = float(cx.max())
            if sx < best_score:
                best_score = sx
                best = x.copy()
                last_improve = 0

        if last_improve > 6000:
            a = best.copy()
            y = best.copy()
            t = 1.0
            last_improve = 0
            noise = rng.normal(0.0, 0.005 * S / math.sqrt(float(n)), n)
            y = project_simplex(y + noise, S)

    best = safe_normalize(best, n, S)
    return best, best_score


def run_fista_toppk(a0, n, S, max_time, rng, top_k_start, top_k_end):
    a = safe_normalize(a0, n, S)
    y = a.copy()
    t = 1.0
    c = fft_convolve_self(a)
    best_score = float(c.max())
    best = a.copy()

    start = time.monotonic()
    it = 0
    last_improve = 0

    while time.monotonic() - start < max_time:
        it += 1
        top_k = int(min(top_k_end, max(top_k_start, top_k_start + it // 250)))

        c, grad = conv_and_grad_toppk(y, top_k)
        if grad.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
            break

        score = float(c.max())
        if score < best_score:
            best_score = score
            best = y.copy()
            last_improve = 0
        else:
            last_improve += 1

        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
        if gnorm < 1e-14:
            break

        step = 2.0 * S / (float(n) * (1.0 + 0.005 * it))
        x = project_simplex(y - step * grad / max(gnorm, 1e-15), S)

        t_new = 0.5 * (1.0 + math.sqrt(1.0 + 4.0 * t * t))
        y = x + ((t - 1.0) / t_new) * (x - a)
        y = np.maximum(y, 0.0)
        sy = float(y.sum())
        if sy > 0.0:
            y *= S / sy
        else:
            y = a.copy()

        a = x
        t = t_new

        if it % 10 == 0:
            cx = fft_convolve_self(x)
            sx = float(cx.max())
            if sx < best_score:
                best_score = sx
                best = x.copy()
                last_improve = 0

        if last_improve > 6000:
            a = best.copy()
            y = best.copy()
            t = 1.0
            last_improve = 0
            noise = rng.normal(0.0, 0.004 * S / math.sqrt(float(n)), n)
            y = project_simplex(y + noise, S)

    best = safe_normalize(best, n, S)
    return best, best_score


def polish_sequence(a_prob, n, deadline, rng):
    S = math.sqrt(2.0 * float(n))
    a0 = safe_normalize(a_prob, n, S)
    best = a0
    best_score = conv_max_scaled(a0)

    rem = max(0.0, float(deadline) - time.monotonic())
    if rem > 60.0:
        seg = min(rem * 0.35, 1200.0)
        a1, s1 = run_fista_lse(a0, n, S, seg, rng, 10.0, 350.0)
        if s1 < best_score:
            best = a1
            best_score = s1
        print("  polish LSE-1 score=%.8f" % best_score)

    rem = max(0.0, float(deadline) - time.monotonic())
    if rem > 40.0:
        seg = min(rem * 0.35, 900.0)
        a1, s1 = run_fista_toppk(best, n, S, seg, rng, 8, 128)
        if s1 < best_score:
            best = a1
            best_score = s1
        print("  polish TopK-1 score=%.8f" % best_score)

    rem = max(0.0, float(deadline) - time.monotonic())
    if rem > 30.0:
        seg = min(rem * 0.50, 1200.0)
        a1, s1 = run_fista_lse(best, n, S, seg, rng, 60.0, 900.0)
        if s1 < best_score:
            best = a1
            best_score = s1
        print("  polish LSE-2 score=%.8f" % best_score)

    rem = max(0.0, float(deadline) - time.monotonic())
    if rem > 20.0:
        seg = min(rem * 0.80, 900.0)
        a1, s1 = run_fista_toppk(best, n, S, seg, rng, 48, 320)
        if s1 < best_score:
            best = a1
            best_score = s1
        print("  polish TopK-2 score=%.8f" % best_score)

    best = safe_normalize(best, n, S)
    return best, best_score


def uniform_sequence(n=8192):
    S = math.sqrt(2.0 * float(n))
    return np.full(int(n), S / float(n), dtype=np.float64)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2380, **kwargs):
    try:
        budget_s = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget_s = 2380.0

    if not np.isfinite(budget_s) or budget_s <= 0.0:
        budget_s = 2380.0
    budget_s = min(budget_s, 2380.0)

    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(5.0, budget_s - 20.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    print("=== Starting cosine-family construction ===")
    plan = [
        (8192, [1, 2, 3, 5]),
        (16384, [1, 2]),
    ]
    candidates = search_cosine_plan(plan, deadline)

    best_score = np.inf
    best_seq = None
    best_n = 0

    for item in candidates:
        if item[0] < best_score:
            best_score = float(item[0])
            best_seq = item[1]
            best_n = int(item[2])

    if best_seq is None:
        print("  fallback to default cosine n=8192")
        best_n = 8192
        best_seq = build_cosine_seq(best_n, 1, 1.0 / math.sqrt(2.0), math.pi / 2.0)
        best_score = obj_fft(best_seq)
        print("  fallback cosine score=%.8f" % best_score)

    # Prefer length 8192 if it is close to the best; final direct convolution is cheaper.
    chosen_score = best_score
    chosen_seq = best_seq
    chosen_n = best_n
    for item in candidates:
        if int(item[2]) == 8192 and float(item[0]) <= chosen_score + 1e-4:
            chosen_score = float(item[0])
            chosen_seq = item[1]
            chosen_n = 8192
            break

    # If the previous SOTA is available and improves things, use it as an alternative start.
    given = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if given is not None:
        try:
            gseq = resize_sequence(given, chosen_n)
            if gseq is not None:
                gscore = obj_fft(gseq)
                if np.isfinite(gscore) and gscore < chosen_score:
                    chosen_seq = gseq
                    chosen_score = float(gscore)
                    print("  previous sequence improved to %.8f" % chosen_score)
        except Exception:
            pass

    print("=== Selected initial sequence n=%d score=%.8f ===" % (chosen_n, chosen_score))

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 40.0:
        polished, polished_score = polish_sequence(chosen_seq, chosen_n, deadline - 5.0, rng)
        if np.isfinite(polished_score) and polished_score < chosen_score:
            chosen_seq = polished
            chosen_score = float(polished_score)
        else:
            chosen_seq = safe_normalize(chosen_seq, chosen_n, math.sqrt(2.0 * float(chosen_n)))
            chosen_score = float(obj_fft(chosen_seq))
        print("=== After polish: n=%d score=%.8f ===" % (chosen_seq.size, chosen_score))

    final_seq = np.asarray(chosen_seq, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    final_n = int(final_seq.size)

    if final_n < 8:
        final_seq = uniform_sequence(8192)
        final_n = 8192

    S_final = math.sqrt(2.0 * float(final_n))
    final_seq = safe_normalize(final_seq, final_n, S_final)
    final_seq = np.clip(final_seq, 0.0, 1000.0)

    if final_seq.size == 0 or float(final_seq.sum()) < 0.01:
        final_seq = uniform_sequence(8192)
        final_n = 8192

    try:
        ver = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in final_seq])
        print("=== evaluate_sequence verification: %.10f ===" % float(ver))
        if not np.isfinite(ver):
            final_seq = uniform_sequence(8192)
    except Exception:
        pass

    return [float(x) for x in final_seq]
```
